[11:35 AM] **+82 10-2770-5862:** [Image] [Munich/Bogenhausen] Room for Sublet (Zwischen) or Replacement Tenant: 3/16 – 8/25 (Anmeldung Possible)
Hi everyone! I’m looking for someone to take over my spot in a great apartment. I had already signed the contract and paid the deposit, but I unexpectedly received a spot in a student dormitory, so I’m urgently looking for a replacement. The location and conditions are excellent—I'm looking for someone who can stay for the entire duration.
🏠 Room Details
• Location: Munich, Bogenhausen (a very safe and clean residential area).
• Size: 26㎡ + includes a private terrace.
• Furniture: Fully furnished (Bed, desk, bookshelf, wardrobe). All existing furniture is available for use.
• Amenities: Elevator in the building.
• Flatshare (WG): 3-person WG. You will be sharing with two very friendly German students (1 female, 1 male).
• Pro-tip: The common areas are kept very clean thanks to a weekly rotating cleaning schedule. The interior was also renovated last year.
📅 Period & Rent
• Period: March 16th – August 25th (Looking for someone who can cover the full dates).
• Warm Rent (Includes heating & internet): €1,025 for 1 person / €1,075 for 2 people.
• Deposit: €1,010.
• Anmeldung: Possible (You can officially register your address here).
📍 Location & Transport
• U-Bahn: U4 Böhmerwaldplatz (7-min walk).
• Tram/Bus: Tram 19 Vogelweideplatz (5-min walk), Bus 59 (3-min walk).
• Proximity:
• 10–15 mins to City Center, Universität, and Münchner Freiheit.
• Approx. 20 mins to LMU (Perfect location for students!).
• Convenience: Rewe, Simmel, Netto, Asian Market, and DM are all within a 5-minute walk.
The current tenant is a very kind Korean person, so the communication and contract process will be very smooth.
Please contact me via WhatsApp for inquiries!
